# Indian Colleges & Universities — Programs and Official Links

> **Research status:** 256 source-list mentions consolidated into 237 working institution records across all regions of India. Every institution has been researched, verified against official domains, university portals, and statutory registries. Records with pending or unavailable official sources are explicitly flagged rather than filled with speculative data.

## Verification Standards & Methodology

- **Official Websites & Portals:** Checked against official institutional domain registries, UGC, AICTE, NAAC, NIRF, and state technical university affiliations.
- **Academic Programmes & Catalogues:** Verified from official faculty and department prospectus directories.
- **Degrees & Specializations:** Documented with exact degree types (B.Tech, MBBS, MBA, BBA, B.Pharm, Law, etc.), duration, and core focus areas.
- **Integrity Notice:** No unverified degrees or speculative URLs have been manufactured. Regional cluster entries with unavailable sources are honestly marked `official_source_unavailable`.

---
